</Info>

## Balances

### get

`balances.get`

Get the account balances per currency, optionally as of a date

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.balances.get({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `as_of_time` | `string` | No | List balances in the response at the date time provided, will return the last refreshed balance in the system when not provided. |
| `currency_code` | `string` | No | Filters the transactions in the response by a [three-character ISO-4217 currency code](/api/rest/reference/currency-codes/) for the PayPal transaction currency. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

## Disputes

### acceptClaim

`disputes.acceptClaim`

Accept claim

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.disputes.acceptClaim({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the dispute for which to provide the supporting information. |
| `accept-claim-document` | `string \| object \| custom` | No | A file with evidence. |
| `input` | `Record<string, any>` | No | The JSON part named `input`: for evidence, `{ evidences: [{ evidence_type, evidence_info, notes, documents }] }`. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="accept-claim-document full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### acceptOffer

`disputes.acceptOffer`

Accept offer to resolve dispute

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.disputes.acceptOffer({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the dispute for which to provide the supporting information. |
| `note` | `string` | No | The customer notes about accepting of offer. PayPal can but the merchant cannot view these notes. |



**Output:** `unknown`

| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the dispute for which to provide the supporting information. |

### list

`disputes.list`

List disputes

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.disputes.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `start_time` | `string` | No | Filters the disputes in the response by a creation date and time. The start time must be within the last 180 days. Value is in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6). For example, *`yyyy`*-*`MM`*-*`dd`*`T`*`HH`*:*`mm`*:*`ss`*.*`SSS`*`Z`.You can specify either but not both the `start_time` and `disputed_transaction_id` query parameters. |
| `disputed_transaction_id` | `string` | No | Filters the disputes in the response by a transaction, by ID.You can specify either but not both the `start_time` and `disputed_transaction_id` query parameter. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | Limits the number of disputes in the response to this value. |
| `next_page_token` | `string` | No | The token that describes the next page of results to fetch. The list disputes call returns this token in the HATEOAS links in the response. |
| `dispute_state` | `string` | No | Filters the disputes in the response by a state. Separate multiple values with a comma (`,`). When you specify more than one dispute_state, the response lists disputes that belong to any of the specified dispute_state. |
| `update_time_before` | `string` | No | The date and time when the dispute was last updated, in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6). For example, *`yyyy`*-*`MM`*-*`dd`*`T`*`HH`*:*`mm`*:*`ss`*.*`SSS`*`Z`. update_time_before must be within the last 180 days and the default is the current time. |
| `update_time_after` | `string` | No | The date and time when the dispute was last updated, in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6). For example, *`yyyy`*-*`MM`*-*`dd`*`T`*`HH`*:*`mm`*:*`ss`*.*`SSS`*`Z`. update_time_after must be within the last 180 days and the default is the maximum time (180 days) supported. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### makeOffer

`disputes.makeOffer`

Make offer to resolve dispute

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.disputes.makeOffer({ id: '5O190127TN364715T', note: 'example', offer_type: 'REFUND' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the dispute for which to provide the supporting information. |
| `note` | `string` | Yes | The merchant's notes about the offer. |
| `offer_amount` | `object` | No | The currency and amount for a financial transaction, such as a balance or payment due. |
| `return_shipping_address` | `object` | No | The portable international postal address. Maps to [AddressValidationMetadata](https://github.com/googlei18n/libaddressinput/wiki/AddressValidationMetadata) and HTML 5.1 [Autofilling form controls: the autocomplete attribute](https://www.w3.org/TR/html51/sec-forms.html#autofilling-form-controls-the-autocomplete-attribute). |
| `invoice_id` | `string` | No | The merchant-provided ID of the invoice for the refund. This optional value maps the refund to an invoice ID in the merchant's system. |
| `offer_type` | `REFUND \| REFUND_WITH_RETURN \| REFUND_WITH_REPLACEMENT \| REPLACEMENT_WITHOUT_REFUND` | Yes | The merchant-proposed offer type for the dispute. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="offer_amount full type">

```ts
{
  currency_code: string,
  value: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="return_shipping_address full type">

```ts
{
  address_line_1?: string,
  address_line_2?: string,
  address_line_3?: string,
  admin_area_4?: string,
  admin_area_3?: string,
  admin_area_2?: string,
  admin_area_1?: string,
  postal_code?: string,
  country_code: string,
  address_details?: {
    street_number?: string,
    street_name?: string,
    street_type?: string,
    delivery_service?: string,
    building_name?: string,
    sub_building?: string
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `unknown`

### requireEvidence

`disputes.requireEvidence`

Update dispute status

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts

```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the dispute for which to provide the supporting information. |
| `action` | `BUYER_EVIDENCE \| SELLER_EVIDENCE` | Yes | The action. Indicates whether the state change enables the customer or merchant to submit evidence. |

### update

`disputes.update`

Partially update dispute

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.disputes.update({
	id: '5O190127TN364715T',
	patch: [{ op: 'add' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the dispute for which to provide the supporting information. |
| `patch` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of JSON patch objects to apply partial updates to resources. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="patch full type">

```ts
{
  op: add | remove | replace | move | copy | test,
  path?: string,
  value?: any,
  from?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `unknown \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
unknown | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

`invoices.accountingConnections`

List Connections


**Output:** `unknown`

`invoices.accountingSync`

List Connection Status for an Invoice

**Risk:** `read`


**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The invoice id of the account. |



**Output:** `unknown`

### cancel

`invoices.cancel`

Cancel sent invoice

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.invoices.cancel({ invoice_id: 'INV2-Z56S-5LLA-Q52L-CPZ5' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invoice_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the draft invoice to delete. |
| `subject` | `string` | No | The subject of the email that is sent as a notification to the recipient.Note: User-provided values for this field will not be honored and the subject will always be defaulted to a system-defined value. |
| `note` | `string` | No | A note to the payer.Note: User-provided values for this field will not be honored and the note will always be defaulted to a system-defined value. |
| `send_to_invoicer` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether to send a copy of the email to the merchant. |
| `send_to_recipient` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether to send a copy of the email to the recipient. |
| `additional_recipients` | `string[]` | No | An array of one or more CC: emails to which notifications are sent. If you omit this parameter, a notification is sent to all CC: email addresses that are part of the invoice.Note: Valid values are email addresses in the `additional_recipients` value associated with the invoice. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |



---

### create

`invoices.create`

Create draft invoice

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.invoices.create({
	detail: { currency_code: 'USD', invoice_number: 'CORS-1001' },
	primary_recipients: [{
		billing_info: { email_address: 'ada@example.com' },
	}],
	items: [{
		name: 'Consulting',
		quantity: '2',
		unit_amount: { currency_code: 'USD', value: '100.00' },
	}],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `detail` | `object` | Yes | The details of the invoice. Includes invoice number, date, payment terms, and audit metadata. |
| `invoicer` | `object` | No | The invoicer business information that appears on the invoice. |
| `primary_recipients` | `object[]` | No | The billing and shipping information. Includes name, email, address, phone and language. |
| `additional_recipients` | `string[]` | No | An array of one or more CC: emails to which notifications are sent. If you omit this parameter, a notification is sent to all CC: email addresses that are part of the invoice.Note: Valid values are email addresses in the `additional_recipients` value associated with the invoice. |
| `items` | `object[]` | No | An array of invoice line item information. |
| `configuration` | `object` | No | The invoice configuration details. Includes partial payment, tip, and tax calculated after discount. |
| `amount` | `object` | No | The invoice amount summary of item total, discount, tax total, and shipping. |
| `payments` | `object` | No | An array of payments registered against the invoice. |
| `refunds` | `object` | No | The invoicing refund details. Includes the refund type, date, amount, and method. |
| `links` | `object[]` | No | An array of request-related [HATEOAS links](/docs/api/reference/api-responses/#hateoas-links). |

### delete

`invoices.delete`

Delete invoice

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.invoices.delete({ invoice_id: 'INV2-Z56S-5LLA-Q52L-CPZ5' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invoice_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the draft invoice to delete. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |



---

### generateQrCode

`invoices.generateQrCode`

Generate QR code

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.invoices.generateQrCode({ invoice_id: 'INV2-Z56S-5LLA-Q52L-CPZ5' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invoice_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the draft invoice to delete. |
| `width` | `number` | No | The width, in pixels, of the QR code image. Value is from `150` to `500`. |
| `height` | `number` | No | The height, in pixels, of the QR code image. Value is from `150` to `500`. |
| `action` | `string` | No | The type of URL for which to generate a QR code. Valid values are `pay` and `details`. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### recordPayment

`invoices.recordPayment`

Record a payment received outside PayPal (cash, check, bank transfer) against an invoice

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.invoices.recordPayment({ invoice_id: 'INV2-Z56S-5LLA-Q52L-CPZ5', method: 'BANK_TRANSFER' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invoice_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the draft invoice to delete. |
| `payment_id` | `string` | No | The ID for a PayPal payment transaction. Required for the `PAYPAL` payment type. |
| `payment_date` | `string` | No | The stand-alone date, in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6). To represent special legal values, such as a date of birth, you should use dates with no associated time or time-zone data. Whenever possible, use the standard `date_time` type. This regular expression does not validate all dates. For example, February 31 is valid and nothing is known about leap years. |
| `method` | `BANK_TRANSFER \| CASH \| CHECK \| CREDIT_CARD \| DEBIT_CARD \| PAYPAL \| WIRE_TRANSFER \| OTHER` | Yes | The payment mode or method through which the invoicer can accept the payments. |
| `note` | `string` | No | A note associated with an external cash or check payment. |
| `amount` | `object` | No | The currency and amount for a financial transaction, such as a balance or payment due. |
| `shipping_info` | `object` | No | The contact information of the user. Includes name and address. |

### remind

`invoices.remind`

Send a payment reminder for an invoice by email

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.invoices.remind({ invoice_id: 'INV2-Z56S-5LLA-Q52L-CPZ5' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invoice_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the draft invoice to delete. |
| `subject` | `string` | No | The subject of the email that is sent as a notification to the recipient.Note: User-provided values for this field will not be honored and the subject will always be defaulted to a system-defined value. |
| `note` | `string` | No | A note to the payer.Note: User-provided values for this field will not be honored and the note will always be defaulted to a system-defined value. |
| `send_to_invoicer` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether to send a copy of the email to the merchant. |
| `send_to_recipient` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether to send a copy of the email to the recipient. |
| `additional_recipients` | `string[]` | No | An array of one or more CC: emails to which notifications are sent. If you omit this parameter, a notification is sent to all CC: email addresses that are part of the invoice.Note: Valid values are email addresses in the `additional_recipients` value associated with the invoice. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |

### search

`invoices.search`

Search for invoices

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.invoices.search({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page` | `number` | No | The page number to be retrieved, for the list of templates. So, a combination of `page=1` and `page_size=20` returns the first 20 templates. A combination of `page=2` and `page_size=20` returns the next 20 templates. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of templates to return in the response. |
| `total_required` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether the to show total_pages and total_items in the response. |
| `recipient_email` | `string` | No | Filters the search by the email address. |
| `recipient_first_name` | `string` | No | Filters the search by the recipient first name. |
| `recipient_last_name` | `string` | No | Filters the search by the recipient last name. |
| `recipient_business_name` | `string` | No | Filters the search by the recipient business name. |
| `invoice_number` | `string` | No | Filters the search by the invoice number. |
| `status` | `(DRAFT \| SENT \| SCHEDULED \| PAID \| MARKED_AS_PAID \| CANCELLED \| REFUNDED \| PARTIALLY_PAID \| PARTIALLY_REFUNDED \| MARKED_AS_REFUNDED \| UNPAID \| PAYMENT_PENDING \| AUTO_CANCELLED \| PAID_EXTERNAL \| REFUNDED_EXTERNAL \| SHARED)[]` | No | An array of status values. |
| `reference` | `string` | No | The reference data. Includes a Purchase Order (PO) number. |
| `currency_code` | `string` | No | The [three-character ISO-4217 currency code](/api/rest/reference/currency-codes/) that identifies the currency. |
| `memo` | `string` | No | A private bookkeeping memo for the user. |
| `total_amount_range` | `object` | No | The amount range. |
| `invoice_date_range` | `object` | No | The date range. Filters invoices by creation date, invoice date, due date, and payment date. |
| `due_date_range` | `object` | No | The date range. Filters invoices by creation date, invoice date, due date, and payment date. |
| `payment_date_range` | `object` | No | The date and time range. Filters invoices by creation date, invoice date, due date, and payment date. |
| `creation_date_range` | `object` | No | The date and time range. Filters invoices by creation date, invoice date, due date, and payment date. |
| `archived` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether to list merchant-archived invoices in the response. Value is:true. Response lists only merchant-archived invoices.false. Response lists only unarchived invoices.null. Response lists all invoices. |
| `fields` | `string[]` | No | A CSV file of fields to return for the user, if available. Because the invoice object can be very large, field filtering is required. Valid collection fields are items, payments, refunds, additional_recipients_info, and attachments. |

### templates.create

`invoices.templates.create`

Create template

| `links` | `object[]` | No | An array of request-related [HATEOAS links](/docs/api/reference/api-responses/#hateoas-links). |

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `template_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the template to delete. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### templates.list

`invoices.templates.list`

List templates

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.invoices.templates.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `fields` | `string` | No | The fields to return in the response. Value is `all` or `none`. To return only the template name, ID, and default attributes, specify `none`. |
| `page` | `number` | No | The page number to be retrieved, for the list of templates. So, a combination of `page=1` and `page_size=20` returns the first 20 templates. A combination of `page=2` and `page_size=20` returns the next 20 templates. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of templates to return in the response. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### templates.update

`invoices.templates.update`

Fully update template

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.invoices.templates.update({ template_id: 'TEMP-19V05281TU309413B' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `template_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the template to delete. |
| `name` | `string` | No | The template name.Note: The template name must be unique. |
| `default_template` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether this template is the default template. A invoicer can have one default template. |
| `template_info` | `object` | No | The template details. Includes invoicer business information, invoice recipients, items, and configuration. |
| `settings` | `object` | No | The template settings. Sets a template as the default template or edit template. |
| `unit_of_measure` | `QUANTITY \| HOURS \| AMOUNT` | No | The unit of measure for the invoiced item. |
| `links` | `object[]` | No | An array of request-related [HATEOAS links](/docs/api/reference/api-responses/#hateoas-links). |

### update

`invoices.update`

Fully update invoice

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.invoices.update({
	invoice_id: 'INV2-Z56S-5LLA-Q52L-CPZ5',
	detail: { currency_code: 'USD' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invoice_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the draft invoice to delete. |
| `send_to_recipient` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether to send the invoice update notification to the recipient. |
| `send_to_invoicer` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether to send the invoice update notification to the merchant. |
| `detail` | `object` | Yes | The details of the invoice. Includes invoice number, date, payment terms, and audit metadata. |
| `invoicer` | `object` | No | The invoicer business information that appears on the invoice. |
| `primary_recipients` | `object[]` | No | The billing and shipping information. Includes name, email, address, phone and language. |
| `additional_recipients` | `string[]` | No | An array of one or more CC: emails to which notifications are sent. If you omit this parameter, a notification is sent to all CC: email addresses that are part of the invoice.Note: Valid values are email addresses in the `additional_recipients` value associated with the invoice. |
| `items` | `object[]` | No | An array of invoice line item information. |
| `configuration` | `object` | No | The invoice configuration details. Includes partial payment, tip, and tax calculated after discount. |
| `amount` | `object` | No | The invoice amount summary of item total, discount, tax total, and shipping. |
| `payments` | `object` | No | An array of payments registered against the invoice. |
| `refunds` | `object` | No | The invoicing refund details. Includes the refund type, date, amount, and method. |
| `links` | `object[]` | No | An array of request-related [HATEOAS links](/docs/api/reference/api-responses/#hateoas-links). |

## Orders

### authorize

`orders.authorize`

Authorize the payment of an approved order (capture it later with payments.authorizations.capture)

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.orders.authorize({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `paypal_request_id` | `string` | No | The server stores keys for 6 hours. The API callers can request the times to up to 72 hours by speaking to their Account Manager. It is mandatory for all single-step create order calls (E.g. Create Order Request with payment source information like Card, PayPal.vault_id, PayPal.billing_agreement_id, etc). |
| `prefer` | `string` | No | The preferred server response upon successful completion of the request. Value is:return=minimal. The server returns a minimal response to optimize communication between the API caller and the server. A minimal response includes the id, status and HATEOAS links.return=representation. The server returns a complete resource representation, including the current state of the resource. |
| `paypal_client_metadata_id` | `string` | No | A Globally Unique Identifier (GUID) value. |
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the order for which to authorize. |
| `paypal_auth_assertion` | `string` | No | Header for an API client-provided JWT assertion that identifies the merchant. Establishing the consent to act-on-behalf of a merchant is a prerequisite for using this header. |
| `payment_source` | `object` | No | — |

**Output:** `(unknown & any) & unknown`


---

### capture

`orders.capture`

Capture the payment of an approved order: money moves from the buyer to you

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.orders.capture({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `paypal_request_id` | `string` | No | The server stores keys for 6 hours. The API callers can request the times to up to 72 hours by speaking to their Account Manager. It is mandatory for all single-step create order calls (E.g. Create Order Request with payment source information like Card, PayPal.vault_id, PayPal.billing_agreement_id, etc). |
| `prefer` | `string` | No | The preferred server response upon successful completion of the request. Value is:return=minimal. The server returns a minimal response to optimize communication between the API caller and the server. A minimal response includes the id, status and HATEOAS links.return=representation. The server returns a complete resource representation, including the current state of the resource. |
| `paypal_client_metadata_id` | `string` | No | A Globally Unique Identifier (GUID) value. |
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the order for which to capture a payment. |
| `paypal_auth_assertion` | `string` | No | Header for an API client-provided JWT assertion that identifies the merchant. Establishing the consent to act-on-behalf of a merchant is a prerequisite for using this header. |
| `payment_source` | `object` | No | — |

**Output:** `(unknown & any) & unknown`


---

### confirmPaymentSource

`orders.confirmPaymentSource`

Confirm the Order

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.orders.confirmPaymentSource({
	id: '5O190127TN364715T',
	payment_source: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `paypal_client_metadata_id` | `string` | No | A Globally Unique Identifier (GUID) value. |
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the order for which the payer confirms their intent to pay. |
| `paypal_auth_assertion` | `string` | No | Header for an API client-provided JWT assertion that identifies the merchant. Establishing the consent to act-on-behalf of a merchant is a prerequisite for using this header. |
| `prefer` | `string` | No | The preferred server response upon successful completion of the request. Value is:return=minimal. The server returns a minimal response to optimize communication between the API caller and the server. A minimal response includes the id, status and HATEOAS links.return=representation. The server returns a complete resource representation, including the current state of the resource. |
| `payment_source` | `object` | Yes | The payment source definition. |
| `application_context` | `object` | No | Customizes the payer confirmation experience. |

</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `(unknown & any) & unknown`


---

### create

`orders.create`

Create a Checkout order (intent CAPTURE or AUTHORIZE) with purchase units, amounts and an optional payment source; the buyer approves it through the payer-action link

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.orders.create({
	intent: 'CAPTURE',
	purchase_units: [{
		reference_id: 'order-1001',
		amount: { currency_code: 'USD', value: '49.00' },
	}],
	paypal_request_id: '7b92603e-77ed-4896-8e78-5dea2050476a',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `paypal_request_id` | `string` | No | The server stores keys for 6 hours. The API callers can request the times to up to 72 hours by speaking to their Account Manager. It is mandatory for all single-step create order calls (E.g. Create Order Request with payment source information like Card, PayPal.vault_id, PayPal.billing_agreement_id, etc). |
| `paypal_partner_attribution_id` | `string` | No | PayPal Partner can send a PayPal-Partner-Attribution-Id request header with the value that they have been assigned by the PayPal Partner program. This value is known as a BN Code. In order to reward such partners (through partner programs), all the activities (including API calls) that they are doing on behalf of the merchants need to be tracked. |
| `paypal_client_metadata_id` | `string` | No | A Globally Unique Identifier (GUID) value. |
| `prefer` | `string` | No | The preferred server response upon successful completion of the request. Value is:return=minimal. The server returns a minimal response to optimize communication between the API caller and the server. A minimal response includes the id, status and HATEOAS links.return=representation. The server returns a complete resource representation, including the current state of the resource. |
| `paypal_auth_assertion` | `string` | No | Header for an API client-provided JWT assertion that identifies the merchant. Establishing the consent to act-on-behalf of a merchant is a prerequisite for using this header. |
| `intent` | `CAPTURE \| AUTHORIZE` | Yes | The intent to either capture payment immediately or authorize a payment for an order after order creation. |
| `payer` | `object` | No | — |
| `purchase_units` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of purchase units. Each purchase unit establishes a contract between a payer and the payee. Each purchase unit represents either a full or partial order that the payer intends to purchase from the payee. |
| `payment_source` | `object` | No | The payment source definition. |
| `application_context` | `object` | No | — |

**Output:** `(unknown & any) & unknown`


---

### get

`orders.get`

Show order details

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.orders.get({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the order for which to show details. |
| `paypal_auth_assertion` | `string` | No | Header for an API client-provided JWT assertion that identifies the merchant. Establishing the consent to act-on-behalf of a merchant is a prerequisite for using this header. |
| `fields` | `string` | No | A comma-separated list of fields that should be returned for the order. Valid filter field is `payment_source`. |



**Output:** `(unknown & any) & unknown`


---

### trackers.create

`orders.trackers.create`

Add tracking information for an Order

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.orders.trackers.create({
	id: '5O190127TN364715T',
	tracking_number: 'example',
	carrier: 'DPD_RU',
	capture_id: '2GG279541U471931P',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the order that the tracking information is associated with. |
| `paypal_auth_assertion` | `string` | No | Header for an API client-provided JWT assertion that identifies the merchant. Establishing the consent to act-on-behalf of a merchant is a prerequisite for using this header. |
| `tracking_number` | `string` | Yes | The tracking number for the shipment. This property supports Unicode. |
| `carrier` | `DPD_RU \| BG_BULGARIAN_POST \| KR_KOREA_POST \| ZA_COURIERIT \| FR_EXAPAQ \| ARE_EMIRATES_POST \| GAC \| GEIS \| SF_EX \| PAGO \| MYHERMES \| DIAMOND_EUROGISTICS \| CORPORATECOURIERS_WEBHOOK \| BOND \| OMNIPARCEL \| SK_POSTA \| PUROLATOR \| FETCHR_WEBHOOK \| THEDELIVERYGROUP \| CELLO_SQUARE \| TARRIVE \| COLLIVERY \| MAINFREIGHT \| IND_FIRSTFLIGHT \| ACSWORLDWIDE PAN_ASIA \| BRT_IT \| SRE_KOREA \| SPEEDEE \| TNT_UK \| VENIPAK \| SHREENANDANCOURIER \| CROSHOT \| NIPOST_NG \| EPST_GLBL \| NEWGISTICS \| POST_SLOVENIA \| JERSEY_POST \| BOMBINOEXP \| WMG \| XQ_EXPRESS \| FURDECO \| LHT_EXPRESS \| SOUTH_AFRICAN_POST_OFFICE \| SPOTON \| DIMERCO \| CYPRUS_POST_CYP \| ABCUSTOM \| IND_DELIVREE \| CN_BESTEXPRESS \| DX_SFTP \| PICKUPP_MYS \| FMX \| HELLMANN \| SHIPBOB \| JERSEYPOST_ATLAS \| CORETRAILS \| RHENUS_ITALY \| JADLOG \| JITSU \| YANWEN_EXPRESS \| DASHLINK \| SEINO_SUPER_EXPRESS \| FLOSHIP \| METROSCG \| SENDPARCEL \| P2P \| CN_EXPRESS \| CIRROTRACK \| LAND_LOGISTICS \| VEHO \| MEDLINE \| VDTRACK \| SINO_SCM \| 3PE_EXPRESS \| SWIFTX \| SFYDEXPRESS \| TOPTRANS \| OTHER` | Yes | The carrier for the shipment. Some carriers have a global version as well as local subsidiaries. The subsidiaries are repeated over many countries and might also have an entry in the global list. Choose the carrier for your country. If the carrier is not available for your country, choose the global version of the carrier. If your carrier name is not in the list, set `carrier` to `OTHER` and set carrier name in `carrier_name_other`. For allowed values, see Carriers. |
| `carrier_name_other` | `string` | No | The name of the carrier for the shipment. Provide this value only if the carrier parameter is OTHER. This property supports Unicode. |
| `capture_id` | `string` | Yes | The PayPal capture ID. |
| `notify_payer` | `boolean` | No | If true, PayPal will send an email notification to the payer of the PayPal transaction. The email contains the tracking details provided through the Orders tracking API request. Independent of any value passed for `notify_payer`, the payer may receive tracking notifications within the PayPal app, based on the user's notification preferences. |
| `items` | `object[]` | No | An array of details of items in the shipment. |

### trackers.update

`orders.trackers.update`

Update or cancel tracking information for an order

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.orders.trackers.update({
	id: '5O190127TN364715T',
	tracker_id: '443844607820',
	patch: [{ op: 'add' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the order that the tracking information is associated with. |
| `tracker_id` | `string` | Yes | The order tracking ID. |
| `paypal_auth_assertion` | `string` | No | Header for an API client-provided JWT assertion that identifies the merchant. Establishing the consent to act-on-behalf of a merchant is a prerequisite for using this header. |
| `patch` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of JSON patch objects to apply partial updates to resources. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="patch full type">

```ts
(
  {
    op: add | remove | replace | move | copy | test,
    path?: string,
    value?: any,
    from?: string
  }& any
)[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |



---

### update

`orders.update`

Update order

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.orders.update({
	id: '5O190127TN364715T',
	patch: [{ op: 'replace', path: '/purchase_units/@reference_id==\'order-1001\'/description', value: 'Corsair hoodie' }],
});
```

|------|------|----------|-------------|

| `paypal_auth_assertion` | `string` | No | Header for an API client-provided JWT assertion that identifies the merchant. Establishing the consent to act-on-behalf of a merchant is a prerequisite for using this header. |
| `patch` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of JSON patch objects to apply partial updates to resources. |

```ts
    op: add | remove | replace | move | copy | test,

  }& any
)[]
```

## Partner Referrals

### create

`partnerReferrals.create`

Create partner referral

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.partnerReferrals.create({
	operations: [{ operation: 'API_INTEGRATION' }],
	legal_consents: [{ type: 'SHARE_DATA_CONSENT', granted: true }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `individual_owners` | `object[]` | No | List of owners in the account. There should be only one primary account owner which is mentioned in their role_type. |
| `business_entity` | `object` | No | — |
| `email` | `string` | No | The internationalized email address.Note: Up to 64 characters are allowed before and 255 characters are allowed after the @ sign. However, the generally accepted maximum length for an email address is 254 characters. The pattern verifies that an unquoted @ sign exists. |
| `preferred_language_code` | `string` | No | The [language tag](https://tools.ietf.org/html/bcp47#section-2) for the language in which to localize the error-related strings, such as messages, issues, and suggested actions. The tag is made up of the [ISO 639-2 language code](https://www.loc.gov/standards/iso639-2/php/code_list.php), the optional [ISO-15924 script tag](https://www.unicode.org/iso15924/codelists.html), and the [ISO-3166 alpha-2 country code](/docs/integration/direct/rest/country-codes/). |
| `tracking_id` | `string` | No | The partner's unique identifier for this customer in their system which can be used to track user in PayPal. |
| `partner_config_override` | `object` | No | The preference to customize the web experience of the customer by overriding that is set at the Partner's Account. |
| `financial_instruments` | `object` | No | Financial instruments attached to this account. |
| `operations` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of operations to perform for the customer while they share their data. |
| `products` | `(ALIPAY \| BANCONTACT \| BLIK \| EPS \| PPCP \| EXPRESS_CHECKOUT \| PAYMENT_METHODS \| ADVANCED_VAULTING \| IDEAL \| MB_WAY \| MULTIBANCO \| PAYPAL_COMMERCE_PLATFORM_BUSINESS \| PPPLUS \| PRZELEWY24 \| SATISPAY \| TRUSTLY \| WECHAT_PAY \| WEBSITE_PAYMENT_PRO \| ZETTLE \| HYPERWALLET_PAYOUTS)[]` | No | An array of PayPal products to which the partner wants to onboard the customer. |
| `capabilities` | `(PAYPAL_WALLET_VAULTING_ADVANCED \| PAY_UPON_INVOICE \| APPLE_PAY \| GOOGLE_PAY)[]` | No | An array of capabilities which the partner wants to enable for the selected products. Supported only when products are specified. |
| `outside_process_dependencies` | `any[]` | No | An array of dependent processes. |
| `legal_consents` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of all consents that the partner has received from this seller. If `SHARE_DATA_CONSENT` is not granted, PayPal does not store customer data. |
| `payout_attributes` | `object` | No | Payout specific attributes. |
| `legal_country_code` | `string` | No | The [two-character ISO 3166-1 code](/docs/api/reference/country-codes/) that identifies the country or region.Note: The country code for Great Britain is GB and not UK as used in the top-level domain names for that country. Use the `C2` country code for China worldwide for comparable uncontrolled price (CUP) method, bank card, and cross-border transactions. |

## Payments

### authorizations.capture

`payments.authorizations.capture`

Capture an authorized payment, in full or for an amount

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.payments.authorizations.capture({ authorization_id: '0VF52814937998046' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `authorization_id` | `string` | Yes | The PayPal-generated ID for the authorized payment to capture. |
| `paypal_request_id` | `string` | No | A unique ID identifying the request header for idempotency purposes. |
| `prefer` | `string` | No | The preferred server response upon successful completion of the request. Value is:return=minimal. The server returns a minimal response to optimize communication between the API caller and the server. A minimal response includes the id, status and HATEOAS links.return=representation. The server returns a complete resource representation, including the current state of the resource. |
| `paypal_auth_assertion` | `string` | No | Header for an API client-provided JWT assertion that identifies the merchant. Establishing the consent to act-on-behalf of a merchant is a prerequisite for using this header. |
| `invoice_id` | `string` | No | The API caller-provided external invoice number for this order. Appears in both the payer's transaction history and the emails that the payer receives. |
| `note_to_payer` | `string` | No | An informational note about this settlement. Appears in both the payer's transaction history and the emails that the payer receives. |
| `amount` | `object` | No | — |
| `final_capture` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether you can make additional captures against the authorized payment. Set to `true` if you do not intend to capture additional payments against the authorization. Set to `false` if you intend to capture additional payments against the authorization. |
| `payment_instruction` | `object` | No | — |
| `soft_descriptor` | `string` | No | The payment descriptor on the payer's account statement. |

<AccordionGroup>

```ts
{
  currency_code: string,
  value: string
}& any
```
</Accordion>

```ts
    {

      }& any,

      }& any
    }& any
```

## Payouts

### create

`payouts.create`

Send money to up to 15,000 recipients (email, phone or PayPal id) in one batch payout

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.payouts.create({
	sender_batch_header: { sender_batch_id: 'batch-2026-11-27', email_subject: 'You have a payout' },
	items: [{
		recipient_type: 'EMAIL',
		amount: { value: '5.00', currency: 'USD' },
		receiver: 'ada@example.com',
		note: 'Thanks!',
	}],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `paypal_request_id` | `string` | No | The server stores keys for 30 days. |
| `sender_batch_header` | `object` | Yes | The sender-provided payout header for a payout request. |
| `items` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of individual payout items. |

```ts
}
```

```ts
  amount: {
    currency: string,
    value: string
  },
  note?: string,
  receiver: string,
  sender_item_id?: string,
  recipient_wallet?: string,
  alternate_notification_method?: {
    phone?: {
      country_code: string,
      national_number: string,
      extension_number?: string
    }
  },
  notification_language?: string,
  application_context?: {
    social_feed_privacy?: string,
    holler_url?: string,
    logo_url?: string
  },

}[]
```

## Plans

### activate

`plans.activate`

Activate plan

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.plans.activate({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the subscription. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |



---

### create

`plans.create`

Create plan

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.plans.create({
	name: 'Example',
	billing_cycles: [{
		frequency: { interval_unit: 'DAY' },
		tenure_type: 'REGULAR',
		sequence: 1,
	}],
	payment_preferences: {},
	product_id: 'PROD-XYAB12ABSB7868434',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `prefer` | `string` | No | The preferred server response upon successful completion of the request. Value is:return=minimal. The server returns a minimal response to optimize communication between the API caller and the server. A minimal response includes the id, status and HATEOAS links.return=representation. The server returns a complete resource representation, including the current state of the resource. |
| `paypal_request_id` | `string` | No | The server stores keys for 72 hours. |
| `product_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the product created through Catalog Products API. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The plan name. |
| `status` | `CREATED \| INACTIVE \| ACTIVE` | No | The initial state of the plan. Allowed input values are CREATED and ACTIVE. |
| `description` | `string` | No | The detailed description of the plan. |
| `billing_cycles` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of billing cycles for trial billing and regular billing. A plan can have at most two trial cycles and only one regular cycle. |
| `payment_preferences` | `object` | Yes | The payment preferences for a subscription. |
| `taxes` | `object` | No | The tax details. |
| `quantity_supported` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether you can subscribe to this plan by providing a quantity for the goods or service. |

<Accordion title="billing_cycles full type">

```ts
{
  pricing_scheme?: {
    fixed_price?: {
      currency_code: string,
      value: string

      starting_quantity: string,
      ending_quantity?: string,
      amount: {
        currency_code: string,
        value: string
      }
    }[]
  },
  frequency: {
    interval_unit: DAY | WEEK | MONTH | YEAR,

}[]
```

```ts
}
```

```ts
  percentage: string,
  inclusive?: boolean
}
```

---

### get

`products.get`

Show product details

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.products.get({ product_id: 'PROD-XYAB12ABSB7868434' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `product_id` | `string` | Yes | The product ID. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### list

`products.list`

List products

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.products.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The number of items to return in the response. |
| `page` | `number` | No | A non-zero integer which is the start index of the entire list of items that are returned in the response. So, the combination of `page=1` and `page_size=20` returns the first 20 items. The combination of `page=2` and `page_size=20` returns the next 20 items. |
| `total_required` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether to show the total items and total pages in the response. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### update

`products.update`

Update product

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.products.update({
	product_id: 'PROD-XYAB12ABSB7868434',
	patch: [{ op: 'add' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `product_id` | `string` | Yes | The product ID. |
| `patch` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of JSON patch objects to apply partial updates to resources. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="patch full type">

```ts
{
  op: add | remove | replace | move | copy | test,
  path?: string,
  value?: number | number | string | boolean | any[] | {
  } | null,
  from?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |



---

## Subscriptions

### activate

`subscriptions.activate`

Activate subscription

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.subscriptions.activate({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the subscription. |
| `reason` | `string` | No | The reason for activation of a subscription. Required to reactivate the subscription. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |



---

### cancel

`subscriptions.cancel`

Cancel a subscription: it stops for good

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.subscriptions.cancel({ id: '5O190127TN364715T', reason: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the subscription. |
| `reason` | `string` | Yes | The reason for the cancellation of a subscription. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |



---

### capture

`subscriptions.capture`

Capture authorized payment on subscription

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.subscriptions.capture({
	id: '5O190127TN364715T',
	note: 'example',
	capture_type: 'OUTSTANDING_BALANCE',
	amount: { currency_code: 'USD', value: '10.00' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `paypal_request_id` | `string` | No | The server stores keys for 72 hours. |
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the subscription. |
| `note` | `string` | Yes | The reason or note for the subscription charge. |
| `capture_type` | `OUTSTANDING_BALANCE` | Yes | The type of capture. |
| `amount` | `object` | Yes | The currency and amount for a financial transaction, such as a balance or payment due. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="amount full type">

```ts
{
  currency_code: string,
  value: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `unknown \| object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
unknown | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### create

`subscriptions.create`

Create subscription

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.subscriptions.create({ plan_id: 'P-5ML4271244454362WXNWU5NQ' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `prefer` | `string` | No | The preferred server response upon successful completion of the request. Value is:return=minimal. The server returns a minimal response to optimize communication between the API caller and the server. A minimal response includes the id, status and HATEOAS links.return=representation. The server returns a complete resource representation, including the current state of the resource. |
| `paypal_request_id` | `string` | No | The server stores keys for 72 hours. |
| `plan_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the plan. |
| `start_time` | `string` | No | The date and time, in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6). Seconds are required while fractional seconds are optional.Note: The regular expression provides guidance but does not reject all invalid dates. |
| `quantity` | `string` | No | The quantity of the product in the subscription. |
| `shipping_amount` | `object` | No | The currency and amount for a financial transaction, such as a balance or payment due. |
| `subscriber` | `object` | No | — |
| `auto_renewal` | `boolean` | No | DEPRECATED. Indicates whether the subscription auto-renews after the billing cycles complete. |
| `application_context` | `object` | No | The application context, which customizes the payer experience during the subscription approval process with PayPal. |
| `custom_id` | `string` | No | The custom id for the subscription. Can be invoice id. |
| `plan` | `object` | No | An inline plan object to customise the subscription. You can override plan level default attributes by providing customised values for the subscription in this object. |
| `merchant_inventory` | `any` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="shipping_amount full type">

```ts
{
  currency_code: string,
  value: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="subscriber full type">

```ts
{
  email_address?: string,
  name?: {
    given_name?: string,
    surname?: string
  },
  phone?: {
    phone_type?: FAX | HOME | MOBILE | OTHER | PAGER,
    phone_number: {
      national_number: string
    }
  },
  shipping_address?: {
    name?: {
      full_name?: string
    },
    type?: SHIPPING | PICKUP_IN_PERSON | PICKUP_IN_STORE | PICKUP_FROM_PERSON,
    options?: {
      id: string,
      label: string,
      type?: SHIPPING | PICKUP | PICKUP_IN_STORE | PICKUP_FROM_PERSON,
      amount?: {
        currency_code: string,
        value: string
      },
      selected: boolean
    }[],
    address?: {
      address_line_1?: string,
      address_line_2?: string,
      admin_area_2?: string,
      admin_area_1?: string,
      postal_code?: string,
      country_code: string
    }
  },
  payment_source?: {
    card?: {
      name?: string,
      number?: string,
      expiry?: string,
      security_code?: string,
      type?: CREDIT | DEBIT | PREPAID | STORE | UNKNOWN,
      brand?: VISA | MASTERCARD | DISCOVER | AMEX | SOLO | JCB | STAR | DELTA | SWITCH | MAESTRO | CB_NATIONALE | CONFIGOGA | CONFIDIS | ELECTRON | CETELEM | CHINA_UNION_PAY | DINERS | ELO | HIPER | HIPERCARD | RUPAY | GE | SYNCHRONY | UNKNOWN,
      billing_address?: {
        address_line_1?: string,
        address_line_2?: string,
        admin_area_2?: string,
        admin_area_1?: string,
        postal_code?: string,
        country_code: string
      },
      attributes?: {
        customer?: {
          id?: string,
          email_address?: string,
          phone?: {
            phone_type?: FAX | HOME | MOBILE | OTHER | PAGER,
            phone_number: {
              national_number: string
            }
          }
        },
        vault?: {
          store_in_vault?: ON_SUCCESS
        },
        verification?: {
          method?: SCA_ALWAYS | SCA_WHEN_REQUIRED | 3D_SECURE | AVS_CVV
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="application_context full type">

```ts
{
  brand_name?: string,
  locale?: string,
  shipping_preference?: GET_FROM_FILE | NO_SHIPPING | SET_PROVIDED_ADDRESS,
  user_action?: CONTINUE | SUBSCRIBE_NOW,
  payment_method?: {
    payee_preferred?: UNRESTRICTED | IMMEDIATE_PAYMENT_REQUIRED
  },
```

```ts
{
```

### get

`subscriptions.get`

Show subscription details

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.subscriptions.get({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the subscription. |
| `fields` | `string` | No | List of fields that are to be returned in the response. Possible value for fields are last_failed_payment and plan. |



**Output:** `((unknown & any) & unknown) & unknown`

### revise

`subscriptions.revise`

Revise plan or quantity of subscription

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.subscriptions.revise({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the subscription. |
| `plan_id` | `string` | No | The unique PayPal-generated ID for the plan. |
| `quantity` | `string` | No | The quantity of the product or service in the subscription. |
| `shipping_amount` | `object` | No | The currency and amount for a financial transaction, such as a balance or payment due. |
| `shipping_address` | `object` | No | The shipping details. |
| `application_context` | `object` | No | The application context, which customizes the payer experience during the subscription approval process with PayPal. |
| `plan` | `object` | No | An inline plan object to customise the subscription. You can override plan level default attributes by providing customised values for the subscription in this object. |

```ts
{
  currency_code: string,
  value: string
```

```ts
  address?: {
    address_line_1?: string,
    address_line_2?: string,
    admin_area_2?: string,
    admin_area_1?: string,
    postal_code?: string,
    country_code: string
  }
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="application_context full type">

```ts
{
  brand_name?: string,
  locale?: string,
  shipping_preference?: GET_FROM_FILE | NO_SHIPPING | SET_PROVIDED_ADDRESS,
  payment_method?: {
    payee_preferred?: UNRESTRICTED | IMMEDIATE_PAYMENT_REQUIRED
  },
  return_url: string,
  cancel_url: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="plan full type">

```ts
{
  billing_cycles?: {

        }

  }[],
```

---

### suspend

`subscriptions.suspend`

Suspend subscription

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.subscriptions.suspend({ id: '5O190127TN364715T', reason: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the subscription. |
| `reason` | `string` | Yes | The reason for suspension of the Subscription. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |



---

### transactions

`subscriptions.transactions`

List transactions for subscription

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.subscriptions.transactions({ id: '5O190127TN364715T', start_time: '2026-11-27T10:00:00Z', end_time: '2026-11-27T10:00:00Z' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the subscription. |
| `start_time` | `string` | Yes | The start time of the range of transactions to list. |
| `end_time` | `string` | Yes | The end time of the range of transactions to list. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### update

`subscriptions.update`

Update subscription

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.subscriptions.update({
	id: '5O190127TN364715T',
	patch: [{ op: 'add' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the subscription. |
| `patch` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of JSON patch objects to apply partial updates to resources. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="patch full type">

```ts
{
  op: add | remove | replace | move | copy | test,
  path?: string,
  value?: any,
  from?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |



---

**Output:** `unknown`


---

### get

`trackers.get`

Show tracking information

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.trackers.get({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the tracker in the transaction_id-tracking_number format. |
| `account_id` | `string` | No | Encrypted PayPal Account ID of the buyer or seller. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### list

`trackers.list`

List tracking information

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.trackers.list({ transaction_id: 'EXTR-86F38350LX4353815' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `transaction_id` | `string` | Yes | Filters the tracking information that appears in the response by a PayPal transaction ID. |
| `tracking_number` | `string` | No | Filters the tracking information that appears in the response by a tracking number. |
| `account_id` | `string` | No | Encrypted PayPal Account ID of the buyer or seller. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### update

`trackers.update`

Update or cancel tracking information for PayPal transaction

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.trackers.update({ id: '5O190127TN364715T', transaction_id: 'EXTR86F38350LX4353815', status: 'CANCELLED' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the tracker in the transaction_id-tracking_number format. |
| `transaction_id` | `string` | Yes | The PayPal transaction ID. |
| `tracking_number` | `string` | No | The tracking number for the shipment. Carrier information, `carrier` is required if tracking_number is provided. |
| `tracking_number_type` | `CARRIER_PROVIDED \| E2E_PARTNER_PROVIDED` | No | The tracking number type. |
| `status` | `CANCELLED \| DELIVERED \| LOCAL_PICKUP \| ON_HOLD \| SHIPPED \| SHIPMENT_CREATED \| DROPPED_OFF \| IN_TRANSIT \| RETURNED \| LABEL_PRINTED \| ERROR \| UNCONFIRMED \| PICKUP_FAILED \| DELIVERY_DELAYED \| DELIVERY_SCHEDULED \| DELIVERY_FAILED \| INRETURN \| IN_PROCESS \| NEW \| VOID \| PROCESSED \| NOT_SHIPPED \| COMPLETED` | Yes | The status of the item shipment. For allowed values, see Shipping Statuses. |
| `shipment_date` | `string` | No | The stand-alone date, in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6). To represent special legal values, such as a date of birth, you should use dates with no associated time or time-zone data. Whenever possible, use the standard `date_time` type. This regular expression does not validate all dates. For example, February 31 is valid and nothing is known about leap years. |
| `carrier` | `2EBOX \| 360LION \| 3JMSLOGISTICS \| 4_72 \| 6LS \| A1POST \| AAA_COOPER \| ABCUSTOM \| ABXEXPRESS_MY \| ACOMMMERCE \| ACSWORLDWIDE \| ACS_GR \| ADERONLINE \| ADICIONAL \| ADSONE \| Carrier information is required when tracking_number is provided. Some carriers have a global version as well as local subsidiaries. The subsidiaries are repeated over many countries and might also have an entry in the global list. Choose the carrier for your country. If the carrier is not available for your country, choose the global version of the carrier. If your carrier name is not in the list, set `carrier` to `OTHER` and set carrier name in `carrier_name_other`. For allowed values, see Carriers. |

| `notify_buyer` | `boolean` | No | If true , sends an email notification to the buyer of the PayPal transaction. The email contains the tracking information that was uploaded through the API. |
| `last_updated_time` | `string` | No | The date and time, in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6). Seconds are required while fractional seconds are optional.Note: The regular expression provides guidance but does not reject all invalid dates. |
| `shipment_direction` | `FORWARD \| RETURN` | No | To denote whether the shipment is sent forward to the receiver or returned back. |
| `tracking_url` | `string` | No | Tracking Link of the shipment. |
| `links` | `object[]` | No | An array of request-related [HATEOAS links](/docs/api/reference/api-responses/#hateoas-links). |

<Accordion title="links full type">

```ts
{
  href: string,
  rel: string,
  method?: GET | POST | PUT | DELETE | HEAD | CONNECT | OPTIONS | PATCH
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |

## Transactions

### list

`transactions.list`

List transactions for up to 31 days, with filters (transaction id or type, status, amount, currency, dates)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.transactions.list({
	start_date: '2026-11-01T00:00:00-07:00',
	end_date: '2026-11-27T23:59:59-07:00',
	fields: 'all',
	page_size: 100,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `transaction_id` | `string` | No | Filters the transactions in the response by a PayPal transaction ID. A valid transaction ID is 17 characters long, except for an order ID, which is 19 characters long.Note: A transaction ID is not unique in the reporting system. The response can list two transactions with the same ID. One transaction can be balance affecting while the other is non-balance affecting. |
| `transaction_type` | `string` | No | Filters the transactions in the response by a PayPal transaction event code. See [Transaction event codes](/docs/integration/direct/transaction-search/transaction-event-codes/). |
| `transaction_status` | `string` | No | Filters the transactions in the response by a PayPal transaction status code. Value is:Status&nbsp;codeDescriptionDPayPal or merchant rules denied the transaction.PThe transaction is pending. The transaction was created but waits for another payment process to complete, such as an ACH transaction, before the status changes to S.SThe transaction successfully completed without a denial and after any pending statuses.VA successful transaction was reversed and funds were refunded to the original sender. |
| `transaction_amount` | `string` | No | Filters the transactions in the response by a gross transaction amount range. Specify the range as ` TO `, where `` is the lower limit of the gross PayPal transaction amount and `` is the upper limit of the gross transaction amount. Specify the amounts in lower denominations. For example, to search for transactions from $5.00 to $10.05, specify `[500 TO 1005]`.Note:The values must be URL encoded. |
| `transaction_currency` | `string` | No | Filters the transactions in the response by a [three-character ISO-4217 currency code](/api/rest/reference/currency-codes/) for the PayPal transaction currency. |
| `start_date` | `string` | Yes | Filters the transactions in the response by a start date and time, in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6). Seconds are required. Fractional seconds are optional. |
| `end_date` | `string` | Yes | Filters the transactions in the response by an end date and time, in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6). Seconds are required. Fractional seconds are optional. The maximum supported range is 31 days. |
| `payment_instrument_type` | `string` | No | Filters the transactions in the response by a payment instrument type. Value is either:CREDITCARD. Returns a direct credit card transaction with a corresponding value.DEBITCARD. Returns a debit card transaction with a corresponding value.If you omit this parameter, the API does not apply this filter. |
| `store_id` | `string` | No | Filters the transactions in the response by a store ID. |
| `terminal_id` | `string` | No | Filters the transactions in the response by a terminal ID. |
| `fields` | `string` | No | Indicates which fields appear in the response. Value is a single field or a comma-separated list of fields. The transaction_info value returns only the transaction details in the response. To include all fields in the response, specify fields=all. Valid fields are:transaction_info. The transaction information. Includes the ID of the PayPal account of the payee, the PayPal-generated transaction ID, the PayPal-generated base ID, the PayPal reference ID type, the transaction event code, the date and time when the transaction was initiated and was last updated, the transaction amounts including the PayPal fee, any discounts, insurance, the transaction status, and other information about the transaction.payer_info. The payer information. Includes the PayPal customer account ID and the payer's email address, primary phone number, name, country code, address, and whether the payer is verified or unverified.shipping_info. The shipping information. Includes the recipient's name, the shipping method for this order, the shipping address for this order, and the secondary address associated with this order.auction_info. The auction information. Includes the name of the auction site, the auction site URL, the ID of the customer who makes the purchase in the auction, and the date and time when the auction closes.cart_info. The cart information. Includes an array of item details, whether the item amount or the shipping amount already includes tax, and the ID of the invoice for PayPal-generated invoices.incentive_info. An array of incentive detail objects. Each object includes the incentive, such as a special offer or coupon, the incentive amount, and the incentive program code that identifies a merchant loyalty or incentive program.store_info. The store information. Includes the ID of the merchant store and the terminal ID for the checkout stand in the merchant store. |
| `balance_affecting_records_only` | `string` | No | Indicates whether the response includes only balance-impacting transactions or all transactions. Value is either:Y. The default. The response includes only balance transactions.N. The response includes all transactions. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The number of items to return in the response. So, the combination of `page=1` and `page_size=20` returns the first 20 items. The combination of `page=2` and `page_size=20` returns the next 20 items. |
| `page` | `number` | No | The zero-relative start index of the entire list of items that are returned in the response. So, the combination of `page=1` and `page_size=20` returns the first 20 items. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

## Vault

### paymentTokens.create

`vault.paymentTokens.create`

Create payment token for a given payment source

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.vault.paymentTokens.create({
	payment_source: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `paypal_request_id` | `string` | No | The server stores keys for 3 hours. |
| `customer` | `object` | No | This object defines a customer in your system. Use it to manage customer profiles, save payment methods and contact details. |
| `payment_source` | `object` | Yes | The payment method to vault with the instrument details. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="customer full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string,
  merchant_customer_id?: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="payment_source full type">

```ts
{
  card?: {
    name?: string,
    number?: string,
    expiry?: string,
    security_code?: string,
    brand?: VISA | MASTERCARD | DISCOVER | AMEX | SOLO | JCB | STAR | DELTA | SWITCH | MAESTRO | CB_NATIONALE | CONFIGOGA | CONFIDIS | ELECTRON | CETELEM | CHINA_UNION_PAY | DINERS | ELO | HIPER | HIPERCARD | RUPAY | GE | SYNCHRONY | EFTPOS | UNKNOWN,
    billing_address?: {
      address_line_1?: string,
      address_line_2?: string,
      admin_area_2?: string,
      admin_area_1?: string,
      postal_code?: string,
      country_code: string
    },
    network_transaction_reference?: any
  },
  token?: {
    id: string,
    type: SETUP_TOKEN
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### paymentTokens.delete

`vault.paymentTokens.delete`

Delete payment token

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.vault.paymentTokens.delete({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | ID of the setup token. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | PayPal accepted the call; it answered with no body (204). |



---

### paymentTokens.get

`vault.paymentTokens.get`

Retrieve a payment token

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.vault.paymentTokens.get({ id: '5O190127TN364715T' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | ID of the setup token. |



**Output:** `unknown`


---

### paymentTokens.list

`vault.paymentTokens.list`

List all payment tokens

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.vault.paymentTokens.list({ customer_id: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `customer_id` | `string` | Yes | A unique identifier representing a specific customer in merchant's/partner's system or records. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | A non-negative, non-zero integer indicating the maximum number of results to return at one time. |
| `page` | `number` | No | A non-negative, non-zero integer representing the page of the results. |
| `total_required` | `boolean` | No | A boolean indicating total number of items (total_items) and pages (total_pages) are expected to be returned in the response. |



**Output:** `unknown`

## Webhooks

### availableEventTypes

`webhooks.availableEventTypes`

List available events

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.webhooks.availableEventTypes({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output:** `unknown`


---

### create

`webhooks.create`

Create webhook

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.webhooks.create({
	url: 'https://example.com/hooks/corsair',
	event_types: [{ name: 'PAYMENT.CAPTURE.COMPLETED' }, { name: 'CHECKOUT.ORDER.APPROVED' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `url` | `string` | Yes | The URL that is configured to listen on `localhost` for incoming `POST` notification messages that contain event information. |
| `event_types` | `object[]` | Yes | An array of events to which to subscribe your webhook. To subscribe to all events, including events as they are added, specify the asterisk wild card. To replace the `event_types` array, specify the asterisk wild card. To list all supported events, list available events. |
| `links` | `object[]` | No | An array of request-related [HATEOAS links](/docs/api/reference/api-responses/#hateoas-links/). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="event_types full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  resource_versions?: string[]
}[]
```
</Accordion>

| `start_time` | `string` | No | Filters the webhook event notifications in the response to those created on or after this date and time and on or before the `end_time` value. Both values are in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6) format. Example: `start_time=2013-03-06T11:00:00Z`. |
| `end_time` | `string` | No | Filters the webhook event notifications in the response to those created on or after the `start_time` and on or before this date and time. Both values are in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6) format. Example: `end_time=2013-03-06T11:00:00Z`. |
| `transaction_id` | `string` | No | Filters the response to a single transaction, by ID. |
| `event_type` | `string` | No | Filters the response to a single event. |

### verifySignature

`webhooks.verifySignature`

Verify a webhook delivery's signature with PayPal (the plugin's webhooks verify offline with PayPal's certificate)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.webhooks.verifySignature({
	auth_algo: 'example',
	cert_url: 'https://example.com/hooks/corsair',
	transmission_id: 'example',
	transmission_sig: 'example',
	transmission_time: '2026-04-09T10:00:00Z',
	webhook_id: '0EH40505U7160970P',
	webhook_event: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `auth_algo` | `string` | Yes | The algorithm that PayPal uses to generate the signature and that you can use to verify the signature. Extract this value from the `PAYPAL-AUTH-ALGO` response header, which is received with the webhook notification. |
| `cert_url` | `string` | Yes | The X.509 public key certificate. Download the certificate from this URL and use it to verify the signature. Extract this value from the `PAYPAL-CERT-URL` response header, which is received with the webhook notification. |
| `transmission_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the HTTP transmission. Contained in the `PAYPAL-TRANSMISSION-ID` header of the notification message. |
| `transmission_sig` | `string` | Yes | The PayPal-generated asymmetric signature. Appears in the `PAYPAL-TRANSMISSION-SIG` header of the notification message. |
| `transmission_time` | `string` | Yes | The date and time of the HTTP transmission, in [Internet date and time format](https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc3339#section-5.6). Appears in the `PAYPAL-TRANSMISSION-TIME` header of the notification message. |
| `webhook_id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the webhook as configured in your Developer Portal account. |
| `webhook_event` | `object` | Yes | A webhook event notification. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="webhook_event full type">

```ts
{
  event_version?: string,
  resource_version?: string,
  resource?: {
  },
  links?: {
    href: string,
    rel: string,
    method?: GET | POST | PUT | DELETE | HEAD | CONNECT | OPTIONS | PATCH
  }[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `unknown`

## Web Profiles

### create

`webProfiles.create`

Create web experience profile

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.paypal.api.webProfiles.create({ paypal_request_id: '7b92603e-77ed-4896-8e78-5dea2050476a', name: 'Example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `paypal_request_id` | `string` | Yes | The server stores keys for three hours. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The web experience profile name. Must be unique for a set of profiles for a merchant. |
| `temporary` | `boolean` | No | Indicates whether the profile persists for three hours or permanently. To persist the profile permanently, set to `false`. To persist the profile for three hours, set to `true`. |
| `flow_config` | `object` | No | The flow configuration parameters. |
| `input_fields` | `object` | No | The input field customization parameters. |
| `presentation` | `object` | No | The style and presentation parameters. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="flow_config full type">

```ts
{
  landing_page_type?: login | billing,
  bank_txn_pending_url?: string,
  user_action?: COMMIT,
  return_uri_http_method?: GET | POST
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="input_fields full type">

```ts
{
  no_shipping?: number,
  address_override?: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="presentation full type">

```ts
{
  brand_name?: string,
  logo_image?: string,
  locale_code?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `unknown`


---
